# EC1 Cash Flow & NPV. The teaching digest.

# SECTION 1: The engine, its version and what it refuses (owned by Associate m01)

Engine version 3.12.0. Every table below is a return value of computeCashFlow, computeBreakevenOilPrice, npv, irr, paybackYears, the PIA rate derivations or the price resolver. Money is USD, volumes are bbl and Mscf, boe uses 6 Mscf per barrel, rates are percent unless a column says otherwise.

Every PIA run below is the engine on its default path, which follows the texts as gazetted: Petroleum Industry Act 2021 (Act No. 6), Official Gazette No. 142, Vol. 108, 27 August 2021; Nigeria Tax Act 2025 (Act No. 7), Official Gazette No. 117, Vol. 112, 26 June 2025, effective 1 January 2026; re-gazetting ordered December 2025, no Certified True Copy read; Petroleum Royalty Regulations 2022 (S.I. No. 73), Official Gazette No. 205, Vol. 109, 22 November 2022; Finance Act 2023, effective 1 May 2023 (s.30). The texts were read on 2026-09-26. The tertiary education tax is left at the statutory rate for each year, and every other input a case states is printed with it. Section 22 lists the engine's own statements about what it approximates, and Section 23 the three figures the texts leave to a stated reading, which this course never grades.

The engine refuses a run rather than emitting a zero-USD answer when the upload cannot be read. The eight refusals the golden publishes, with the message each one throws:

- no_volume_columns: Ingestion validation failed: Production file: no oil/gas/condensate volume columns recognized. Headers found: date, oil_production. Expected per-well columns ending in _oil_bbl / _gas_mscf / _condensate_bbl, or bare oil_bbl / gas_mscf / condensate_bbl.
- no_usable_date: Ingestion validation failed: Production file: no row had a usable date. Provide a "year", "date" (YYYY-MM or YYYY-MM-DD), or "month_index" column.
- capex_no_cost_column: Ingestion validation failed: CAPEX file: no cost column recognized. Headers found: category, date, spend. Expected one of amount_usd / cost_usd / capex_usd / total_capex_usd / value_usd (or any *_usd column).
- opex_no_cost_column: Ingestion validation failed: OPEX file: no cost column recognized. Headers found: date, monthly_cost. Expected one of total_opex_usd / opex_usd / cost_usd / amount_usd (or any *_usd column).
- oil_price_unset: Ingestion validation failed: Oil price (oil_price_usd_bbl) is not set but the production data has oil volumes.
- gas_price_unset: Ingestion validation failed: Gas price (gas_price_usd_mscf) is not set but the production data has gas volumes.
- ambiguous_cost_aliases: Ingestion validation failed: CAPEX file: a row has multiple cost columns populated with different values (amount_usd, cost_usd). Keep exactly one cost column per row so the amount is unambiguous.
- no_production_rows: No production data found. Upload and process a CSV first.

- allowance_cap_midyear: A new shallow-water PML with 99,000,000 bbl produced before the year, so the year crosses the 100,000,000 bbl new-lease allowance cap. Run with the stated new-PML hydrocarbon tax rate 30 (a stated reading; Section 23).
  The published config is REFUSED on the default path: A new-acreage petroleum mining lease onshore or in shallow water needs pia_new_pml_hct_rate_pct set to 15 or 30; got null. PIA s.267 (NTA s.72) gives 30% to leases selected under s.93(6)(b) and (7)(b) and 15% to onshore and shallow water and to petroleum prospecting licences, and does not say which applies to a lease granted after the Act out of new acreage, so the rate is a stated user choice with no default. Run with pia_new_pml_hct_rate_pct=30.

- pia_marginal_field_blend: A producing marginal field converted under PIA s.94(1) at 8,000 bopd. The published config enters the terrain as marginal_field, which the engine refuses; the digest runs it onshore with pia_marginal_field_pre_2021 true.
  The published config is REFUSED on the default path: pia_terrain "marginal_field" is not a terrain under the Petroleum Industry Act 2021: a marginal field is onshore or in shallow water (PIA Seventh Schedule para 10(4); Petroleum Royalty Regulations 2022 r.13(2)). Set pia_terrain to "onshore" or "shallow_water", and set pia_marginal_field_pre_2021 to true for a producing marginal field converted under PIA s.94(1). Set pia_legacy_pre_audit to true to reproduce a pre-audit run. Run with pia_terrain=onshore.

# SECTION 8: The NPV profile, and the point that misses (owned by Professional m03, Expert m05)

The engine reports NPV at 0, 5, 8, 10, 12, 15 and 20 percent plus the applied rate. The header says the applied point always passes through the headline NPV. The engine LABELS that point with the applied rate rounded to two decimals and EVALUATES it at the exact applied rate, so it passes through the headline NPV on a real basis whose Fisher rate is not a round number too; every gap below is 0.00. THE ROWS CARRY THE POOL BUT NOT THE RECOVERY. A PSC row reports royalty, taxable income (the contractor profit oil), tax, net cash flow and psc_cost_pool_after, the cost still carried at the year end, and kpis.psc_unrecovered_cost_at_cessation reports the pool left when the field stops; no column prints how much cost was recovered in a year. The recovery below is read by marching the engine's own applyPSC over the rows the engine produced, year by year, with the carried amount handed forward exactly as computeCashFlow does internally; the marched pool equals psc_cost_pool_after on every row.

- cost oil cap 30 percent: NPV -104151944.05, IRR -21.3161 percent, payback Beyond project life, take 120.4874 percent, tax by year 26370036.00, 22618299.06, 19329476.12, 16536055.05, 14142143.08, 12175217.49, 10393930.24; cost recovered by year (applyPSC march) 50228640.00, 43082474.40, 36818049.74, 31497247.72, 26937415.39, 23190890.46, 19797962.36; pool carried at each year end 183771360.00, 210408885.60, 199052435.86, 193780636.14, 193855432.19, 198487119.51, 207346412.26.

# SECTION 14: The PIA royalties (owned by Expert m02)

### deriveOilRoyaltyRate: the production royalty on crude oil and condensate, by terrain and daily rate

The daily rate is the year's crude oil plus condensate over the calendar days of the year. Onshore and shallow water: the first 5,000 bopd at 5 percent, the next 5,000 at 7.5 percent, everything above 10,000 bopd at the terrain rate (15 percent onshore, 12.5 percent shallow water), as ONE weighted average rate on the whole volume. Deep offshore: 5 percent up to and including 50,000 bopd, 7.5 percent on the share above, as one weighted average. Frontier: 7.5 percent at every rate. Below, the rate the engine returns, as a fraction:

- onshore: 1 bopd gives 0.050000; 1000 bopd gives 0.050000; 4999 bopd gives 0.050000; 5000 bopd gives 0.050000; 5001 bopd gives 0.050005; 6000 bopd gives 0.054167; 7500 bopd gives 0.058333; 8000 bopd gives 0.059375; 9999 bopd gives 0.062499; 10000 bopd gives 0.062500; 10001 bopd gives 0.062509; 12000 bopd gives 0.077083; 20000 bopd gives 0.106250; 40000 bopd gives 0.128125; 49999 bopd gives 0.132500; 50000 bopd gives 0.132500; 50001 bopd gives 0.132500; 60000 bopd gives 0.135417; 100000 bopd gives 0.141250; 120000 bopd gives 0.142708.
- shallow_water: 1 bopd gives 0.050000; 1000 bopd gives 0.050000; 4999 bopd gives 0.050000; 5000 bopd gives 0.050000; 5001 bopd gives 0.050005; 6000 bopd gives 0.054167; 7500 bopd gives 0.058333; 8000 bopd gives 0.059375; 9999 bopd gives 0.062499; 10000 bopd gives 0.062500; 10001 bopd gives 0.062506; 12000 bopd gives 0.072917; 20000 bopd gives 0.093750; 40000 bopd gives 0.109375; 49999 bopd gives 0.112500; 50000 bopd gives 0.112500; 50001 bopd gives 0.112500; 60000 bopd gives 0.114583; 100000 bopd gives 0.118750; 120000 bopd gives 0.119792.

- marginal_field is not a terrain: pia_terrain "marginal_field" is not a terrain under the Petroleum Industry Act 2021: a marginal field is onshore or in shallow water (PIA Seventh Schedule para 10(4); Petroleum Royalty Regulations 2022 r.13(2)). Set pia_terrain to "onshore" or "shallow_water", and set pia_marginal_field_pre_2021 to true for a producing marginal field converted under PIA s.94(1). Set pia_legacy_pre_audit to true to reproduce a pre-audit run.

### deriveGasRoyaltyRate: natural gas and NGL, every terrain alike, with the in-country share

- in-country share 0 percent: onshore 0.050000; shallow_water 0.050000; deep_offshore 0.050000; frontier 0.050000.
- in-country share 25 percent: onshore 0.043750; shallow_water 0.043750; deep_offshore 0.043750; frontier 0.043750.
- in-country share 50 percent: onshore 0.037500; shallow_water 0.037500; deep_offshore 0.037500; frontier 0.037500.
- in-country share 100 percent: onshore 0.025000; shallow_water 0.025000; deep_offshore 0.025000; frontier 0.025000.

### priceRoyaltyBenchmarks and derivePriceRoyaltyRate: the royalty by price

The rate is 0 at or below the low benchmark, 5 percent at the middle one, 10 percent at or above the high one, and linear between; frontier acreage pays none. The benchmarks move every 1 January by 2 percent of the previous year's benchmark, rounded to whole cents. The Regulations start the escalation from 2021 levels (the engine's default, regulations_2021); the Act reads the same levels as 2020 levels (act_2020). Section 23 treats the difference as a stated reading.

### deriveHctRate: the hydrocarbon tax rate

Arguments: terrain, licence, marginal field converted under s.94(1), override, framework, deep offshore reading, custom rate, lease status, new-PML rate. A rate the texts do not settle is refused until the user states it.

- shallow_water PML converted, PIA year: 0.300000
- onshore PML converted, NTA year: 0.300000
- shallow_water PPL, PIA year: 0.150000
- onshore PML, marginal field converted under s.94(1): 0.150000
- shallow_water new-acreage PML, no rate stated: REFUSED: A new-acreage petroleum mining lease onshore or in shallow water needs pia_new_pml_hct_rate_pct set to 15 or 30; got null. PIA s.267 (NTA s.72) gives 30% to leases selected under s.93(6)(b) and (7)(b) and 15% to onshore and shallow water and to petroleum prospecting licences, and does not say which applies to a lease granted after the Act out of new acreage, so the rate is a stated user choice with no default.
- shallow_water new-acreage PML, stated 30: 0.300000
- shallow_water new-acreage PML, stated 15: 0.150000
- deep_offshore PML, PIA year: 0.000000
- deep_offshore PML, NTA year, no reading stated: REFUSED: A deep offshore year under the Nigeria Tax Act 2025 needs pia_deep_offshore_hct_interpretation set to "conservative_zero", "aggressive_pml_30" or "custom": NTA s.65(1) applies hydrocarbon tax to deep offshore operations but s.72 states rates only for onshore and shallow water, so the rate is a stated user choice with no default.
- deep_offshore PML, NTA year, conservative_zero: 0.000000
- deep_offshore PML, NTA year, aggressive_pml_30: 0.300000
- deep_offshore PML, NTA year, custom 12.5: 0.125000

# SECTION 15: The hydrocarbon tax cascade (owned by Expert m03)

Order the engine applies in a year: royalties (production, price, gas); HCDT (3 percent of the previous year's opex); NDDC (3 percent of the total annual budget, opex plus capex, unless a case states a fixed amount or the opex base); the cost price ratio cap on the costs claimed against the hydrocarbon tax only (65 percent of crude plus condensate revenue by default), with any excess deferred; the production allowance; the hydrocarbon tax on the chargeable profit; companies income tax on its own base, which deducts full opex, royalties, HCDT, NDDC and its own capital allowance and does not deduct the hydrocarbon tax; then TET (PIA years) or the development levy (NTA years). Deductions shared between streams enter the hydrocarbon tax at the crude-plus-condensate share of gross revenue.

### computeProductionAllowance on its own

Converted lease: the lower of 2.50 USD/bbl and 20 percent of the price on every barrel. New lease: the lower of 8.00 USD/bbl and 20 percent up to the cumulative cap (onshore 50, shallow water 100, deep offshore and frontier 500 million bbl), then the lower of 4.00 USD/bbl and 20 percent on every later barrel; a year that crosses the cap is split at the cap. A new lease in deep offshore or frontier has none in an NTA year. eligible_bbl is every barrel the allowance is computed on; below_cap_bbl and after_cap_bbl split it.

- converted lease, 1000000 bbl at 80: allowance 2500000.00, eligible 1000000.00 bbl, below cap 1000000.00 bbl, after cap 0.00 bbl, cap applied false.
- converted lease, 1000000 bbl at 10 (the 20 percent limit binds): allowance 2000000.00, eligible 1000000.00 bbl, below cap 1000000.00 bbl, after cap 0.00 bbl, cap applied false.
- converted lease, 1000000 bbl at 12.5: allowance 2500000.00, eligible 1000000.00 bbl, below cap 1000000.00 bbl, after cap 0.00 bbl, cap applied false.
- new shallow lease, 1000000 bbl at 80, prior 0: allowance 8000000.00, eligible 1000000.00 bbl, below cap 1000000.00 bbl, after cap 0.00 bbl, cap applied false.
- new shallow lease, 1000000 bbl at 80, prior 99000000: allowance 8000000.00, eligible 1000000.00 bbl, below cap 1000000.00 bbl, after cap 0.00 bbl, cap applied false.
- new shallow lease, 1000000 bbl at 80, prior 99500000: allowance 6000000.00, eligible 1000000.00 bbl, below cap 500000.00 bbl, after cap 500000.00 bbl, cap applied true.
- new shallow lease, 1000000 bbl at 80, prior 100000000: allowance 4000000.00, eligible 1000000.00 bbl, below cap 0.00 bbl, after cap 1000000.00 bbl, cap applied true.
- new shallow lease, 1000000 bbl at 30, prior 0: allowance 6000000.00, eligible 1000000.00 bbl, below cap 1000000.00 bbl, after cap 0.00 bbl, cap applied false.
- new shallow lease, 1000000 bbl at 15, prior 100000000: allowance 3000000.00, eligible 1000000.00 bbl, below cap 0.00 bbl, after cap 1000000.00 bbl, cap applied true.
- new onshore lease, 1000000 bbl at 80, prior 49600000: allowance 5600000.00, eligible 1000000.00 bbl, below cap 400000.00 bbl, after cap 600000.00 bbl, cap applied true.
- new deep offshore lease, 1000000 bbl at 80, prior 0, PIA year: allowance 8000000.00, eligible 1000000.00 bbl, below cap 1000000.00 bbl, after cap 0.00 bbl, cap applied false.
- new deep offshore lease, 1000000 bbl at 80, prior 0, NTA year: allowance 0.00, eligible 0.00 bbl, below cap 0.00 bbl, after cap 0.00 bbl, cap applied false.
- new shallow lease, 1000000 bbl at 80, prior 0, NTA year: allowance 8000000.00, eligible 1000000.00 bbl, below cap 1000000.00 bbl, after cap 0.00 bbl, cap applied false.

### Capital allowance: five years, fixed by the texts

capitalAllowanceFraction by year of life (0 to 5): PIA years 0.200000, 0.200000, 0.200000, 0.200000, 0.190000, 0.000000; NTA years 0.200000, 0.200000, 0.200000, 0.200000, 0.200000, 0.000000.
- pia_capex_recovery_years 1: REFUSED: pia_capex_recovery_years is 1, but the PIA Fifth Schedule para 17(1) and NTA First Schedule Part II para 14(1) fix the capital allowance at five years (20, 20, 20, 20, 19 percent under the PIA; 20 percent a year under the NTA). Leave it unset or 5, or set pia_legacy_pre_audit to true to reproduce a pre-audit run with a different recovery life.
- pia_capex_recovery_years 2: REFUSED: pia_capex_recovery_years is 2, but the PIA Fifth Schedule para 17(1) and NTA First Schedule Part II para 14(1) fix the capital allowance at five years (20, 20, 20, 20, 19 percent under the PIA; 20 percent a year under the NTA). Leave it unset or 5, or set pia_legacy_pre_audit to true to reproduce a pre-audit run with a different recovery life.
- pia_capex_recovery_years 5: NPV 141236909.83
- pia_capex_recovery_years 10: REFUSED: pia_capex_recovery_years is 10, but the PIA Fifth Schedule para 17(1) and NTA First Schedule Part II para 14(1) fix the capital allowance at five years (20, 20, 20, 20, 19 percent under the PIA; 20 percent a year under the NTA). Leave it unset or 5, or set pia_legacy_pre_audit to true to reproduce a pre-audit run with a different recovery life.

# SECTION 16: TET or the development levy: the framework by year (owned by Expert m04)

The framework is chosen for EACH year of assessment: with the override on auto, a year before 2026 is a PIA year (tertiary education tax on the CIT assessable profit, no development levy) and a year from 2026 is an NTA year (the development levy, no TET). A ledger that starts in 2025 and runs into 2026 therefore carries both. force_pia and force_nta apply one framework to every year.

statutoryTetRatePct by year: 2020: 2.5; 2021: 2.5; 2022: 2.5; 2023: 3; 2024: 3; 2025: 3.
fiscalFrameworkForYear with auto: 2024: pia_only; 2025: pia_only; 2026: nta_2025; 2027: nta_2025; with force_pia 2030: pia_only; with force_nta 2025: nta_2025.

# SECTION 22: The texts, the engine's own statements, and the engine's published PIA cases (owned by Expert m02 to Expert m06)

### The texts the engine follows, with the date they were read

- pia: Petroleum Industry Act 2021 (Act No. 6), Official Gazette No. 142, Vol. 108, 27 August 2021
- nta: Nigeria Tax Act 2025 (Act No. 7), Official Gazette No. 117, Vol. 112, 26 June 2025, effective 1 January 2026; re-gazetting ordered December 2025, no Certified True Copy read
- regs: Petroleum Royalty Regulations 2022 (S.I. No. 73), Official Gazette No. 205, Vol. 109, 22 November 2022
- fa2023: Finance Act 2023, effective 1 May 2023 (s.30)
- read_on: 2026-09-26

### The engine's statements (kpis.pia_notes), numbered as the tables above cite them

- N1: Royalty by price uses the Petroleum Royalty Regulations 2022 Schedule: 50, 100 and 150 USD/bbl apply to 2021 and rise by 2% of the previous year's benchmark every 1 January from 2022, rounded to whole cents. The Act (PIA Seventh Schedule para 11(1), restated in NTA Seventh Schedule para 6(3)) applies the same levels to 2020 and escalates from 1 January 2021, one year earlier. Set pia_price_royalty_base to "act_2020" for the Act's reading.
- N2: The Regulations' benchmark table prints the 100 USD level as 102.00, 104.00, 106.00, 108.00 and 110.00 for 2022 to 2026, which does not follow its own 2% rule; the engine applies the rule (104.04 in 2023).
- N3: Royalty tranches read the year's crude oil plus condensate divided by the calendar days of the year; the Regulations (r.12(2)) divide each month's production by the days oil was produced in that month.
- N4: Before 2026 the companies income tax capital allowance is limited to two thirds of the assessable profit, with the excess carried forward (CITA Second Schedule para 24(7) as substituted by Finance Act 2023 s.9(b), effective 1 May 2023). The wording in force before 1 May 2023 was not read; the engine applies the same restriction to every year before 2026. Companies in upstream or midstream gas operations are exempt: set pia_cit_company_gas_operations to true.
- N5: The realised oil and condensate prices stand in for the Commission's fiscal prices (PIA Seventh Schedule para 8), so the additional tax at the fiscal price (PIA s.268; NTA s.73) is not computed.
- N6: Nigeria Tax Act 2025 figures follow Official Gazette No. 117, Vol. 112, of 26 June 2025. The National Assembly ordered the Acts re-gazetted in December 2025; no Certified True Copy was read.
- N7: Opex, HCDT, NDDC, capital allowances and any decommissioning contribution enter the hydrocarbon tax at the crude-plus-condensate share of gross revenue. The Act allocates associated-gas costs to crude oil (s.260(2)) and excludes non-associated gas condensate from the tax (s.260(1)(b)(ii)); the engine cannot tell the two gases apart.
- N8: The minimum effective tax rate top-up is a project-level approximation of NTA s.57: the Act tests the company (a member of a multinational group, or turnover of 20 billion naira or more) on audited profit before tax less 5% of depreciation and personnel cost, which a project model cannot see. The top-up is applied only to years under the NTA and is reported on its own line.
- N9: Royalty by price uses the Act's reading (PIA Seventh Schedule para 11(1)): 50, 100 and 150 USD/bbl apply to 2020 and rise by 2% of the previous year's benchmark every 1 January from 2021, rounded to whole cents. The Petroleum Royalty Regulations 2022 Schedule starts the same levels one year later, in 2021.

# SECTION 23: Three figures the texts leave to a stated reading (never graded)

Each is an input the engine refuses to default, or a default it names in its statements. A lesson may show every reading side by side; no capstone field and no keyed question depends on which reading is chosen.

1. The royalty-by-price base year. The Regulations Schedule starts the benchmarks at 2021 levels; the Act (Seventh Schedule para 11(1)) at 2020 levels. The engine defaults to the Regulations and names the Act's reading. Worked example: royalties 199158351.81 on the Regulations base and 197047101.45 on the Act's; NPV 141236909.83 and 142018072.46.
2. Deep offshore hydrocarbon tax in an NTA year. NTA s.65(1) brings deep offshore into the tax and s.72 prints rates only for onshore and shallow water, so the engine refuses to run an NTA deep offshore year until a reading is stated. The same 60,000 bopd field under force_nta: conservative_zero: HCT 0.00, NPV 892772585.37; aggressive_pml_30: HCT 426107993.35, NPV 466664592.02; custom 12.5: HCT 177544997.23, NPV 715227588.14.
3. The hydrocarbon tax rate of a new-acreage PML onshore or in shallow water. PIA s.267 (NTA s.72) gives 30 percent to leases selected on conversion and 15 percent to onshore and shallow water and to petroleum prospecting licences, and does not say which applies to a lease granted after the Act out of new acreage. The engine refuses the run until 15 or 30 is stated. allowance_cap_midyear at each: 30: HCT 33098197.72, NPV 18637168.50; 15: HCT 16549098.86, NPV 34850773.29.

End of digest.
